- **animate-it**: Animation implementation protocol for web interfaces: easing, duration, springs, CSS transitions, keyframes, Motion (Framer Motion), WAAPI. Fires on three branches: adding motion to a component ("animate this", "should this animate?"); diagnosing motion that feels wrong ("make this transition smoother", "what easing should I use"); and reviewing pasted animation code.
